# PASS 2 -- `lunaabsentcpu` (#503): the DIFF and its detector.  Tree `GXEMUL-SEC/src/`, HEAD `83df95c`.  2026-09-06 ~09:50.

## CONTEXT AND VOCABULARY
An ordinary correctness review of an open-source machine emulator's board model (GXemul 0.7.0, hardened fork): `src/devices/dev_luna88k.c`,
the OMRON LUNA-88K interrupt controller model.  The "guest" is the operating system inside the emulator.  Plain engineering vocabulary:
"the emulator process exits", "a slot that was never connected", "a bounded walk".  **If you cannot read files that is expected -- every
fact is inlined; name any premise you cannot settle rather than guessing.**  Tree under review: `GXEMUL-SEC/src/` (three pristine-2021 copies
under the project root are NOT this fork).  **VERDICT LINE FIRST.**  Cite by grep anchor.  `python3` in git-bash is a Windows Store stub; a
file-reading seat that needs Python uses `C:\Users\User00\AppData\Local\Programs\Python\Python312\python.exe`.

## READ THIS FIRST -- PLEASE DO NOT SIMPLY RATIFY
Check the premises, not just the derivations.  This brief's author has already been wrong once in this round (the filing said "#438's probe
stores to MASK1 with bit 26 clear"; 0x84000100 has bit 26 SET -- the probe missed the defect because nothing was pending and it never
stored SOFT_INT1).  Where a claim below is checkable, check it.

## THE DEFECT (measured, HEAD 83df95c)
`reassert_interrupts()` walks all `MAX_CPUS` (4) slots and calls `INTERRUPT_ASSERT(d->cpu_irq[cpu])` -- a bare call through a function
pointer -- but DEVINIT connects `cpu_irq[1..3]` only for `ncpus >= 2/3/4`; on the default ONE-cpu luna-88k the pointers are the memset's
NULL.  Two ordinary supervisor stores -- `INT_ST_MASK1 <- 0xfc000000` then `SOFT_INT1 <- 1` -- make the walk find `status & enable`
non-zero on slot 1 with `irqActive[1] == 0` and call NULL: the emulator dies with SIGSEGV.  The SIO device line 5 (bit 30; a WR1 TX_IE
store to 0x51000004) reaches the same walk with NO software-interrupt store.  Witness, rung 3, unmodified `-e luna-88k`:
`regress/artifacts/lunaabsentcpu/probe_v2_head.out` -- rows `soft1` and `siofirst` HALT "HOST DIED (signal 11)"; 15/17 (the denominator
drops from 22 because the host dies mid-session and the remaining rows of that session cannot run).

## SETTLED PREMISES, RE-INLINED (each a QUESTION if you can check it)
1. The walk (anchor `static void reassert_interrupts`): per slot `status = d->interrupt_status` (machine-wide: the six shared device lines,
   `1 << (line + 25)`, lines 1..6 -> bits 26..31), `enable = d->interrupt_enable[cpu]`, `if (d->software_interrupt_status[cpu]) status |=
   (1 << 26)`, then `if (status & enable) { if (!d->irqActive[cpu]) INTERRUPT_ASSERT(d->cpu_irq[cpu]); d->irqActive[cpu] = 1; } else {
   if (d->irqActive[cpu]) INTERRUPT_DEASSERT(d->cpu_irq[cpu]); d->irqActive[cpu] = 0; }`.
2. `INTERRUPT_ASSERT(istruct)` is `(istruct).interrupt_assert(&(istruct))` -- no NULL check (src/include/interrupt.h).
3. DEVINIT: `memset(d, 0, ...)`; `INTERRUPT_CONNECT(devinit->interrupt_path, d->cpu_irq[0])`; then under `// HACK for trying out CPUs 1, 2
   and 3`: `if (devinit->machine->ncpus >= 2) INTERRUPT_CONNECT("machine[0].cpu[1]", d->cpu_irq[1]);` and likewise `>= 3` / `>= 4`.  Later
   in DEVINIT an `ncpus > 4` check exits the process at init time (so the walk never runs with ncpus > MAX_CPUS).
4. The store arms index by ADDRESS (`cpunr = (addr - INT_ST_MASK0) / 4`, likewise SOFT_INT0..3) and never compare with ncpus; both call
   `reassert_interrupts(d)` after the store.  `case RESET_CPU_ALL:` in the same switch DOES bound by `cpu->machine->ncpus`.
5. Delivery is observable only free-running (`cpu_dyntrans.c`: no interrupts while single-stepping): the probe parks the cpu on `br .`,
   breakpoints VBR+8 (the INTERRUPT vector), and `continue`s.  A cpu entering VBR+8 with PSR 0x8000000b / EPSR 0x80000000 is the channel.
6. Pass-1 rulings (nine seats; Opus measured 15 binaries, Fable ruled the option set): fix = option (a) ALONE -- bound the WALK to the
   connected-slot count captured at DEVINIT; the absent slots' MASK/SOFT registers stay guest-writable storage with readback (a board-model
   question, filed `lunaabsentmaskdecl`); the SIO line-5 row is MANDATORY; readbacks are asserted, not only survival.  Fable also proposed an
   alternative (F4): bound the reassert CALL in the two store arms and leave the walk untouched -- built as mutant M_CALLGUARD below.

## THE DIFF, VERBATIM (the fix as it will land in `GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_luna88k.c`; derived from SEC by the fix script and measured
## in a scratch clone -- SEC does not carry it yet because round #502 is uncommitted and shares the record files)

## THE DETECTOR (`regress/luna_absentcpu_probe.py` v2, frozen md5 5a133aaa; its own header, verbatim, ends this section)
Rows: session A (one cpu, stepped): live, devsig, slot0, mask1, soft1, sio5, sio5clr, mask2, mask3, live2; session A2: siofirst (the
TX_IE store FIRST, no SOFT store); session B (one cpu, free-running): cpu0 setup, slot0 deliver (pc == VBR+8), slot0 shadow; session C
(`-n 2`): two cpus, cpu1 setup, mask1 land, survive, cpu1 deliver (cpu 1 enters VBR+8), cpu1 shadow, cpu0 parked; session D (`-n 2`,
nothing pending): nodeliver ctl.  22 rows; `LUNAABS_RESULT=<ok>/<rows>`, `LUNA_ABSENTCPU_WITNESS=PASS|PRE_FIX_SYMPTOM|FAIL`, `LUNAABS_DONE`.
Gate wiring (to land with the fix, beside the #438 luna probe in `gate_m88k_rounding.sh`): run it with `"$PMAX" "$IMAGES"`, pin
`LUNAABS_DONE`, `LUNA_ABSENTCPU_WITNESS=PASS`, `LUNAABS_RESULT=22/22`, and the named rows `soft1`, `siofirst`, `cpu1 deliver`,
`nodeliver ctl` OK.

```
lunaabsentcpu (#503) -- rung-3 WITNESS and DETECTOR for dev_luna88k.c reassert_interrupts().

THE DEFECT.  reassert_interrupts() walked all MAX_CPUS (4) slots and called INTERRUPT_ASSERT(d->cpu_irq[cpu]) --
a direct call through the struct's function pointer -- while DEVINIT connected cpu_irq[1..3] only under
`ncpus >= 2/3/4' and memset()s the device to zero.  The INT_ST_MASK0-3 and SOFT_INT0-3 store arms take their slot
from the ADDRESS (`cpunr = (addr - INT_ST_MASK0) / 4') with no ncpus bound, so on the default ONE-cpu luna-88k a
guest word store of 0xfc000000 to INT_ST_MASK1 (0x65000004) arms slot 1, and a following store of 1 to SOFT_INT1
(0x69000004) sets that slot's pending bit and calls reassert SYNCHRONOUSLY inside the store: a NULL function
pointer is called and the emulator process dies with SIGSEGV (MEASURED 2026-09-05: signal 11, no diagnostic).
Any device line whose bit the armed slot's mask enables kills the same way on a running guest -- the clock is line
6 (bit 31); the SIO TX-interrupt route (line 5, bit 30) is SYNCHRONOUS and guest-drivable while stepping (session A2).

THE FIX (#503, option (a) of the pass-1 set): `d->ncpus' captured at DEVINIT and the walk bounded `cpu < d->ncpus'.
The absent slots' registers stay guest-writable storage with readback (a MODEL question filed as lunaabsentmaskdecl).

ONE cold-debugger session per SESSION below, real m88k guest instructions (st/ld through real address decode and
real memory_rw), COMMITTED UNMODIFIED machine description (`-e luna-88k' + the committed rig image; `-n 2' for the
two-cpu accept rows is a command-line configuration of the same description, not a machine edit).  EVERY PLANTED
WORD IS DISASSEMBLED AND ITS TEXT CHECKED BEFORE IT IS STEPPED.

  session A  (1 cpu, stepped)          row          CHANNEL: the mutant it kills / the observable that differs
    C1  RAM word through the same decode            live         identity (floor c): 0x12345678 back
    C2  MASK0 <- 0xfc000000; ld = 0x00fc0000        devsig       RAM-vs-device: the handler's own >>8
    A1  SOFT_INT0 <- 1 on the CONNECTED slot 0      slot0        attribution: the same two stores survive; readback 1
    A2  MASK1 <- 0xfc000000 ALONE; ld = 0x00fc0000  mask1        option-(b)-shaped edits (a declined store reads 0)
    W1  SOFT_INT1 <- 1: THE WITNESS                 soft1        M_REVERT/M_OFFBY1/M_NULLA/M_LATCH/M_GUARDASSERT: host alive.
                                                                 TWO channels in one row -- the STORE exercises ASSERT, the
                                                                 READBACK (== 1; read clears the pending bit -> the next
                                                                 reassert takes the DEASSERT arm) exercises DEASSERT.
                                                                 Three mutants die on the readback, not the store.
    S1  SIO cmd <- 0x01 then 0x02 (WR1 TX_IE): a    sio5         M_CALLGUARD (bound the reassert CALL in the store arms,
        device line (5, bit 30) asserts and walks                loop untouched: W1 survives, this one still walks slot 1).
        the loop with slot 1 armed; ld MASK1 =                   The measured form of "the next enabled device line kills".
        0xa0fc0000 (level 5 in 31..29, mask in 23..16)
    S2  SIO cmd <- 0x28 (RESET_TX_IP), then WR1 <- 0 sio5clr     the DEASSERT walk with a line pending on slot 1 (an
        ld MASK1 = 0x00fc0000                                    ASSERT-only NULL guard dies here); level back to 0
    M2  MASK2 <- 0x84000100; ld = 0x00840001 (23..0) mask2       es438cells: the third label stores and reads back
    M3  MASK3 <- 0x84000100; ld = 0x00840001         mask3       es438cells: the fourth label
    C3  RAM again                                   live2        the machine still executes after everything above
  session A2 (1 cpu, stepped)  MASK1 armed then S1 FIRST: the SIO route's own pre-fix death, ordered before any SOFT store
    sio-first                                        siofirst     PRE-FIX: HALT at the TX_IE store (line 5 walks slot 1)
  session B  (1 cpu, FREE-RUNNING)  MASK0 <- 0x04000000 (bit 26 ONLY: no device line can raise it), SOFT_INT0 <- 1,
        VBR <- 0x40000 (RAM), PSR <- 0x80000000 (IND CLEAR), `br .' planted at VBR+8, breakpoint there, `continue'
    cpu0 setup / slot0 deliver / slot0 shadow        M_NOASSIGN (ncpus never assigned -> 0: the loop never runs, slot 0
                                                     is never asserted, every SURVIVAL row is green) and M_NOREASSERT2:
                                                     cpu 0 enters its INTERRUPT vector (pc == 0x40008), PSR 0x8000000b,
                                                     EPSR 0x80000000.  Stepping cannot deliver (`if (!single_step)').
  session C  (`-n 2', FREE-RUNNING)  cpu 1: VBR 0x40000, PSR 0x80000000, parked at `br .'; cpu 0 steps MASK1 <-
        0x04000000 and SOFT_INT1 <- 1, parks; breakpoint VBR+8; `continue'
    two cpus / cpu1 setup / mask1 land / survive /   M_HARD1 (`d->ncpus = 1' hardcoded -- the clause-(d) null repair:
    cpu1 deliver / cpu1 shadow / cpu0 parked         a TOTAL ESCAPE of every 1-cpu row, measured 20/20): cpu 1 enters
                                                     its vector (pc == 0x40008), IND set, cpu 0 still parked.
  session D  (`-n 2', control)  the same without the SOFT_INT1 store: NO vector entry within the wait
    nodeliver ctl                                    makes the two delivery rows attributable

RESULT words per row: OK / HALT (the host died: signal / exit) / BADREGS (a value assert failed) / STOP (no vector entry
before the wait ran out) / NOSTART.  Output: `LUNAABS_ROW=<name> RESULT=<word> ...' per row, `LUNAABS_RESULT=<ok>/<rows>',
`LUNA_ABSENTCPU_WITNESS=PASS|PRE_FIX_SYMPTOM|FAIL ...', `LUNAABS_DONE'.  Exit status is non-zero on any row that is not OK
(a gate can rely on it).  On HEAD the witness reads PRE_FIX_SYMPTOM at row soft1 (session A) and at siofirst (session A2).

CONTRACT (clause e): catches the walk bound reverted or off by one, an ASSERT-only NULL guard, a decline-once latch on
either arm, a guard moved inside the branch, the reassert CALL bounded in the store arms instead of the walk (sio5), the
bound never assigned (session B), the bound hardcoded to 1 (session C), a declined store to an absent slot (mask1/mask2/
mask3 readbacks), and the third/fourth MASK labels.  Does NOT catch: a both-sided NULL guard instead of the bound
(EQUIVALENT on every reachable configuration -- measured 20/20 + 8/8; the choice is warrant-based), a deleted reassert
in the SOFT arm alone (the clock's own reassert carries the delivery; disclosed debt), the clock/timer route (host-timer
driven, not steppable -- same loop as sio5), MASK1..3 written by a real guest kernel (no luna88k source on disk).

Encodings (cpu_m88k.c, the same source as regress/luna_intmask_probe.py):  ld rD,rS1,IMM16 op26=0x05 ; st rD,rS1,IMM16
op26=0x09 ; br . = c0000000.
usage: luna_absentcpu_probe.py <gxemul-binary> <images-dir>
```

store with nothing pending walks slot 1 without calling anything); M_CALLGUARD was predicted equivalent and is killed.

## CONTRACT (what the detector claims and does not)
Catches: the walk reaching an unconnected slot by any route (device line, software interrupt, mask store), the bound being absent, one
too large, hardcoded, or applied to one call side only, a latch-then-deliver, and the alternative call-site fix; pins delivery to slot 0
(one cpu) and slot 1 (two cpus) with the vector entry and PSR/EPSR, and that nothing is delivered with nothing pending.  Does NOT catch:
a both-sided NULL guard (equivalent on every reachable configuration -- disclosed); the SOFT arm's redundant reassert (M_NOREASSERT: the
clock's reassert carries delivery -- disclosed debt); the ncpus > 4 configuration (DEVINIT exits at init time -- out of the guest's
reach); the absent slots' register semantics (storage with readback, as upstream; filed lunaabsentmaskdecl).

## QUESTIONS
Q1 THE DIFF: is it right and complete?  The capture `d->ncpus = devinit->machine->ncpus` sits right after the memset and BEFORE the
   existing `ncpus > 4` exit (Fable pass 1 said capture AFTER that exit or clamp) -- is the order material, given that exit ends the process
   at init time?  Is there any caller of reassert_interrupts() -- the SIO line, the clock tick, the two store arms -- for which the bound is
   wrong?  Is a walk bound the right member of the six-membered option set versus a decline of the STORES to absent slots (the pass-1 ruling
   said (a) alone; M_CALLGUARD now measures why the call-site form is insufficient)?
Q2 THE FLOOR: does a clean-sweep mutant exist in the (d) DEPENDS-ON enumeration of the shipped composite -- the predicate `cpu < d->ncpus`
   and the fields the walk's body reads (interrupt_status, interrupt_enable[cpu], software_interrupt_status[cpu], irqActive[cpu],
   cpu_irq[cpu]) -- with joint cells where the body tests them ({slot} x {pending} x {irqActive})?  Give the exact edit and the row that
   should have caught it, or CLOSE with your own denominator.
Q3 THE RECORDS: the ledger row `lunaabsentcpu` (HIGH, open) and its OUTSTANDING_BUGS block name option (a) alone and the cpu-1-asserting
   accept channel; the CHANGELOG #503 block is not yet written.  What must the block carry beyond the table above (the M_CALLGUARD finding,

## OPERATIONAL RULE FOR EVERY SEAT (appended by panel.sh, not by the author)

**Do not run `run.sh`, any `gate_*.sh`, or the battery.** Every measurement you need is
inlined above. A gate takes minutes -- longer than your response budget -- and a second
concurrent invocation corrupts the operator run in progress, because the gates share one
log directory. A seat that runs a gate produces no review AND may invalidate someone
else s. Building and running a single detector or mutant in your own temp directory is
fine and encouraged; driving the harness is not.

If you want a measurement that is not inlined, NAME IT and say why. Naming it is worth
far more to this panel than a timeout.

static void add_to_sio_queue(struct luna88k_data* d, int n, uint8_t c)
{
	d->sio_queue[n][d->sio_queue_tail[n]++] = c;
	if (d->sio_queue_tail[n] >= SIO_QUEUE_SIZE)
		d->sio_queue_tail[n] = 0;

	if (d->sio_queue_tail[n] == d->sio_queue_head[n])
		fatal("[ luna88k: add_to_sio_queue overrun; increase SIO_QUEUE_SIZE ]\n");
}

static void reassert_interrupts(struct luna88k_data *d)
{
	int cpu;

	for (cpu = 0; cpu < MAX_CPUS; cpu++) {
		// printf("cpu%i: status = 0x%08x, enable = 0x%08x\n",
		//	cpu, d->interrupt_status, d->interrupt_enable[0]);

		// General interrupt status is for the entire machine,
		// but the Software interrupts (IPIs) are per CPU.
		uint32_t status = d->interrupt_status;
		uint32_t enable = d->interrupt_enable[cpu];

		if (d->software_interrupt_status[cpu])
			status |= (1 << 26);

	        if (status & enable) {
			if (!d->irqActive[cpu])
		                INTERRUPT_ASSERT(d->cpu_irq[cpu]);

			d->irqActive[cpu] = 1;
		} else {
			if (d->irqActive[cpu])
		                INTERRUPT_DEASSERT(d->cpu_irq[cpu]);

			d->irqActive[cpu] = 0;
		}
	}

	// printf("--\n");
}

static void luna88k_interrupt_assert(struct interrupt *interrupt)
{
        struct luna88k_data *d = (struct luna88k_data *) interrupt->extra;
	d->interrupt_status |= (1 << (interrupt->line + 25));
	reassert_interrupts(d);
}

static void luna88k_interrupt_deassert(struct interrupt *interrupt)
{
        struct luna88k_data *d = (struct luna88k_data *) interrupt->extra;
	d->interrupt_status &= ~(1 << (interrupt->line + 25));
	reassert_interrupts(d);
}

static void reassert_timer_interrupt(struct luna88k_data* d)
{
	if (d->pending_timer_interrupts)
		INTERRUPT_ASSERT(d->timer_irq);
	else
		INTERRUPT_DEASSERT(d->timer_irq);
}

static void reassert_serial_interrupt(struct luna88k_data* d)
{
	int assertSerial = 0;
	int port;

	for (port = 0; port <= 1; port++) {
		if ((d->obio_sio_wr[port][SCC_WR1] & SCC_WR1_RXI_ALL_CHAR) ||
		    (d->obio_sio_wr[port][SCC_WR1] & SCC_WR1_RXI_FIRST_CHAR)) {
			if (anything_in_sio_queue(d, port))
				assertSerial = 1;
		}

		if (d->obio_sio_wr[port][SCC_WR1] & SCC_WR1_TX_IE &&
		    d->sio_tx_pending[port])
			assertSerial = 1;
	}

	if (assertSerial)
		INTERRUPT_ASSERT(d->sio_irq);
	else
		INTERRUPT_DEASSERT(d->sio_irq);
}


/*
 *  luna88k_timer_tick():
 *
 *  This function is called LUNA88K_PSEUDO_TIMER_HZ times per real-world second.
 */
static void luna88k_timer_tick(struct timer *t, void *extra)
{
	struct luna88k_data* d = (struct luna88k_data*) extra;
	d->pending_timer_interrupts ++;
	
	// More than a second lost? Then restart the nr of pending interrupts.
	if (d->pending_timer_interrupts > (int)(LUNA88K_PSEUDO_TIMER_HZ)) {
		d->pending_timer_interrupts = 1;

		debugmsg(SUBSYS_DEVICE, "luna88k", VERBOSITY_DEBUG,
		    "Timer ticks lost... Host too slow?");
	}
}


int luna88k_lcd_access(struct luna88k_data *d, int writeflag, int idata)
{
	int odata = 0;

	switch (d->pio1c) {

	case 0x00:
	case 0xb0:
		// Ignore for now.
		break;

	case 0x90:	// "control"
		if (writeflag && ((idata & 0xc0) == 0xc0 || (idata & 0xc0) == 0x80)) {
			d->lcd_x = idata & 0x0f;
			d->lcd_y = idata & 0x40 ? 1 : 0;
		} else if (writeflag) {
			switch (idata) {

			case 0x01:
				// CLS
				memset(d->lcd, ' ', sizeof(d->lcd));
				d->lcd_x = 0;
				d->lcd_y = 0;
				break;

			case 0x02:
				// HOME
				d->lcd_x = 0;
				d->lcd_y = 0;
				break;

			case 0x06:
				// ENTRY
				break;

			case 0x0c:
				// ON
				break;

			case 0x38:
				// INIT
				break;

	/*  This is also a 88200, revision 9:  */
	cmmu->reg[CMMU_IDR] = (M88200_ID << 21) | (9 << 16);
	cmmu->batc[8] = BATC8;
	cmmu->batc[9] = BATC9;
	snprintf(tmpstr, sizeof(tmpstr), "m8820x addr=0x%x addr2=%i", daddr, 1 + 2 * cpunr);
	device_add(devinit->machine, tmpstr);
}


DEVINIT(luna88k)
{
	char n[100];
	struct luna88k_data *d;

	CHECK_ALLOCATION(d = (struct luna88k_data *) malloc(sizeof(struct luna88k_data)));
	memset(d, 0, sizeof(struct luna88k_data));

	// TODO: Values should correspond to the bootable disk id!
	const int nsymbols = 2;
	const char *s[2] = { "boot_unit", "boot_partition" };
	const char *v[2] = { "0", "0" };
	int base = 0x80; // 0x20 for luna-88k2
	for (int si = 0; si < nsymbols; ++si) {
		for (size_t i = 0; i < strlen(s[si]); ++i)
			d->nvram[base + 4 * (32*si + i)] = s[si][i];
		for (size_t i = 0; i < strlen(v[si]); ++i)
			d->nvram[base + 4 * (32*si + 16 +i)] = v[si][i];
	}

	memset(d->lcd, ' ', sizeof(d->lcd));

	memory_device_register(devinit->machine->memory, devinit->name,
	    LUNA88K_REGISTERS_BASE, LUNA88K_REGISTERS_LENGTH, dev_luna88k_access, (void *)d,
	    DM_DEFAULT, NULL);

	/*
	 *  Connect to the CPU's interrupt pin, and register
	 *  6 hardware interrupts:
	 */
	INTERRUPT_CONNECT(devinit->interrupt_path, d->cpu_irq[0]);

	// HACK for trying out CPUs 1, 2 and 3. TODO: Base on devinit->interrupt_path instead.
	if (devinit->machine->ncpus >= 2)
		INTERRUPT_CONNECT("machine[0].cpu[1]", d->cpu_irq[1]);
	if (devinit->machine->ncpus >= 3)
		INTERRUPT_CONNECT("machine[0].cpu[2]", d->cpu_irq[2]);
	if (devinit->machine->ncpus >= 4)
		INTERRUPT_CONNECT("machine[0].cpu[3]", d->cpu_irq[3]);

        for (int i = 1; i <= 6; i++) {
                struct interrupt templ;
                snprintf(n, sizeof(n), "%s.luna88k.%i", devinit->interrupt_path, i);

                memset(&templ, 0, sizeof(templ));
                templ.line = i;
                templ.name = n;
                templ.extra = d;
                templ.interrupt_assert = luna88k_interrupt_assert;
                templ.interrupt_deassert = luna88k_interrupt_deassert;

		// debug("registering irq: %s\n", n);

                interrupt_handler_register(&templ);
        }

	/*  Timer.  */
	snprintf(n, sizeof(n), "%s.luna88k.6", devinit->interrupt_path);
	INTERRUPT_CONNECT(n, d->timer_irq);
	d->timer = timer_add(LUNA88K_PSEUDO_TIMER_HZ, luna88k_timer_tick, d);

	machine_add_tickfunction(devinit->machine, dev_luna88k_tick, d, TICK_STEPS_SHIFT);

	/*
	 *  IRQ 5,4,3 (?): "autovec" according to OpenBSD
	 *
	 *  5 = sio0 (serial controller)
	 *  4 = le0  (lance ethernet)
	 *  3 = spc0 (SCSI)
	 */
	snprintf(n, sizeof(n), "%s.luna88k.5", devinit->interrupt_path);
	INTERRUPT_CONNECT(n, d->sio_irq);

	d->console_handle = console_start_slave(devinit->machine, "SIO", 1);
	devinit->machine->main_console_handle = d->console_handle;

	if (devinit->machine->ncpus > 4) {
		printf("LUNA 88K can't have more than 4 CPUs.\n");
		exit(1);
	}

	add_cmmu_for_cpu(devinit, 0, CMMU_I0, CMMU_D0);
	add_cmmu_for_cpu(devinit, 1, CMMU_I1, CMMU_D1);
	add_cmmu_for_cpu(devinit, 2, CMMU_I2, CMMU_D2);
	add_cmmu_for_cpu(devinit, 3, CMMU_I3, CMMU_D3);

	// The address is a hack because dev_le assumes that the data and register ports
	// are at offset 0x100000 and "ram" (used for packets) is at 0x000000.
	snprintf(n, sizeof(n), "%s.luna88k.4", devinit->interrupt_path);
	dev_le_init(devinit->machine, devinit->machine->memory, LANCE_ADDR - 0x100000, 0, 0, n, DEV_LE_LENGTH);

	// TODO: actual address from the le device!
	// According to OpenBSD's if_le.c, the format for
	// real Luna machines is 00000Axxxxxx.
	const char *enaddr = "ENADDR=00000A102030";
	size_t i = 0;
	while (enaddr[i]) {
		d->fuse_rom[i*2+0] = (uint32_t)(enaddr[i] & 0xf0) << 24;
		d->fuse_rom[i*2+1] = (uint32_t)(enaddr[i] & 0x0f) << 28;
		++i;
	}

	snprintf(n, sizeof(n), "mb89352 addr=0xE1000000 irq=%s.luna88k.3", devinit->interrupt_path);
	device_add(devinit->machine, n);

	if (devinit->machine->x11_md.in_use) {
		d->using_framebuffer = true;
		device_add(devinit->machine, "lunafb addr=0xB1000000");
	}

	return 1;
}

def row(name, result, note=""):            d = os.read(self.fd, 65536)        while time.time() - t < timeout:
            if not self.rd():
                return False
            resp = self.buf[mark:]
            if echo is not None and echo not in resp:
                continue
            if len(self.buf) > mark and resp.rstrip().endswith("GXemul>"):
                return True
        return False

    def send(self, x, timeout=60):
        b = (x + "\n").encode("latin1")
        mark = len(self.buf)
        n = 0
        try:
            while n < len(b):
                n += os.write(self.fd, b[n:])
        except OSError:
            self.dead = True
            return False
        return self.wait(mark=mark, echo=x if x else None, timeout=timeout)

    def reg(self, name, cpu=None, cr=False):
        """Read GPR/pc (`reg [cpu]') or a control register (`reg [cpu],c')."""
        mark = len(self.buf)
        cmd = "reg"
        if cpu is not None:
            cmd += " %d" % cpu
        if cr:
            cmd += ",c"
        if not self.send(cmd, timeout=30):
            return None
        m = re.search(r"\b%s\s*=\s*0x([0-9a-fA-F]+)" % re.escape(name), self.buf[mark:])
        return int(m.group(1), 16) if m else None

    def step_at(self, addr):
        if self.dead:
            return False
        if not self.send("pc=0x%08x" % addr):
            return False
        ok = self.send("step 1", timeout=30)
        return ok and not self.dead

    def host_status(self):
        try:
            _, st = os.waitpid(self.pid, os.WNOHANG)
            if os.WIFSIGNALED(st):
                return "signal %d" % os.WTERMSIG(st)
            if os.WIFEXITED(st):
                return "exit %d" % os.WEXITSTATUS(st)
        except Exception as e:
            return "unknown (%s)" % e
        return "still running?"

    def plant(self, words):
        for addr, word in words:
            self.send("put w 0x%08x, 0x%08x" % (addr, word))

    def check_dis(self):
        """Disassemble every planted program slot and compare with the emulator's own spelling."""
        ok = True
        for addr, word, want in SLOTS:
            mark = len(self.buf)
            self.send("unassemble 0x%08x" % addr)
            m = re.search(r"((?:ld|st)[\w.]*\s+r\d+,r\d+,0x[0-9a-f]+)", self.buf[mark:])
            got = m.group(1) if m else "(none)"
            good = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", got) == re.sub(r"\s+", " ", want)
            print("  0x%08x word=0x%08x dis=%-20r %s" % (addr, word, got, "OK" if good else "*** WRONG ENCODING ***"))
            ok = ok and good
        return ok

    def cont_until_breakpoint(self, timeout):
        """`continue' and wait for the BREAKPOINT line; returns True on a hit, False on timeout (after ^C)."""
        mark = len(self.buf)
        try:
            os.write(self.fd, b"continue\n")
        except OSError:
            self.dead = True
            return False
        t = time.time()
        while time.time() - t < timeout:
            if not self.rd(0.3):
                return False
            if "BREAKPOINT" in self.buf[mark:]:
                self.wait(mark=len(self.buf) - 1, timeout=15)
                return True
        try:
            os.write(self.fd, b"\x03")
        except OSError:
            self.dead = True
            return False
        self.wait(mark=len(self.buf), timeout=15)
        return False

    def close(self):
        try:
            os.write(self.fd, b"quit\n")
            time.sleep(0.3)
            os.kill(self.pid, 9)
            os.waitpid(self.pid, 0)
        except Exception:
            pass


def boot(ncpus=1):
    s = Sess(ncpus)
    if not s.wait(timeout=180):
        print("NO-PROMPT")
        return None
    s.plant([(a, w) for a, w, _ in SLOTS] + [(PARK, SELF), (VBR + 8, SELF)])
    if not s.check_dis():
        s.close()
        return None
    s.send("r6=0x%08x" % SCRATCH)
    return s


def value(s, label, got, want, mask=0xffffffff):
    ok = got is not None and (got & mask) == want
    print("  %-62s got=%s want=0x%08x %s" % (label, ("0x%08x" % got) if got is not None else "DEAD/NONE", want,
                                            "OK" if ok else "*** MISMATCH ***"))
    return ok


def store(s, addr, val):
    """Word store of VAL to ADDR through the planted `st r4,r5,0'; False if the host died."""
    s.send("r5=0x%08x" % addr)
    s.send("r4=0x%08x" % val)
    return s.step_at(A_WDEV)


def load(s, addr):
    s.send("r5=0x%08x" % addr)
    s.send("r4=0x00000000")
    if not s.step_at(A_RDEV):
        return None
    return s.reg("r4")


def died(s, name, note):
    st = s.host_status()
    m = re.findall(r"\[[^\r\n]*luna88k[^\r\n]*\]", s.buf)
    row(name, "HALT", "HOST DIED (%s); last device line: %s -- %s" % (st, m[-1] if m else "(none)", note))

def session_A():
    print("=== SESSION A (1 cpu, stepped): the witness rows, then the SIO line-5 route, then MASK2/MASK3")
    s = boot(1)
    if s is None:
        row("live", "NOSTART", "no prompt / bad encoding"); return
    s.send("r4=0x12345678"); s.step_at(A_WRAM); s.send("r4=0x00000000"); s.step_at(A_RRAM)
    row("live", "OK" if value(s, "C1 RAM readback", s.reg("r4"), 0x12345678) else "BADREGS")
    if not store(s, MASK0, 0xfc000000):
        died(s, "devsig", "died on a LEGAL MASK0 store -- the probe itself is broken"); s.close(); return
    g = load(s, MASK0)
    if g is None:
        died(s, "devsig", "died on a LEGAL MASK0 read"); s.close(); return
    row("devsig", "OK" if value(s, "C2 read INT_ST_MASK0 after write 0xfc000000", g, 0x00fc0000, 0x00ffffff) else "BADREGS")
    # A1: the CONNECTED slot 0 -- assert AND deassert (the readback clears the pending bit)
    if not store(s, SOFT0, 1):
        died(s, "slot0", "host died asserting the CONNECTED cpu 0 -- attribution control FAILED"); s.close(); return
    g = load(s, SOFT0)
    if g is None:
        died(s, "slot0", "host died on the SOFT_INT0 readback (the DEASSERT walk on the connected slot)"); s.close(); return
    row("slot0", "OK" if value(s, "A1 SOFT_INT0 readback (the stored 1; the read clears it)", g, 1) else "BADREGS")
    # A2: arm the ABSENT slot 1; nothing pending yet
    if not store(s, MASK0 + 4, 0xfc000000):

    g = load(s, MASK0 + 4)
    if g is None:

    # W1: THE WITNESS -- store, then the readback (the DEASSERT walk)

        s.close(); return
    g = load(s, SOFT0 + 4)
    if g is None:
        died(s, "soft1", "W1 readback: the DEASSERT walk (read clears the pending bit) reached NULL -- an ASSERT-only guard"); s.close(); return
    row("soft1", "OK" if value(s, "W1 SOFT_INT1 store survived; readback (the stored 1, read clears it)", g, 1) else "BADREGS")
    # S1: the SIO line-5 route -- a DEVICE line asserts with slot 1 armed (mask bit 30) and walks the loop
    if not store(s, SIOCMD0, 0x01):
        died(s, "sio5", "died selecting WR1 (the deassert walk with nothing pending)"); s.close(); return
    if not store(s, SIOCMD0, 0x02):
        died(s, "sio5", "S1: WR1 TX_IE -> sio_tx_pending -> line 5 asserts -> reassert walks slot 1 (mask bit 30) -> NULL"); s.close(); return
    g = load(s, MASK0 + 4)
    if g is None:
        died(s, "sio5", "died on the MASK1 readback after the line-5 assert"); s.close(); return
    row("sio5", "OK" if value(s, "S1 MASK1 readback: level 5 in 31..29 (line 5 pending) + mask in 23..16", g, 0xa0fc0000) else "BADREGS")
    # S2: disarm -- RESET_TX_IP (the deassert walk with a line pending), then WR1 <- 0

    store(s, SIOCMD0, 0x01); store(s, SIOCMD0, 0x00)
    g = load(s, MASK0 + 4)

def session_A2():

def session_B():

        died(s, "cpu0 setup", "died arming/asserting the connected slot 0"); s.close(); return
    s.send("VBR=0x%08x" % VBR); s.send("PSR=0x80000000"); s.send("pc=0x%08x" % PARK)

    ok = value(s, "B VBR set", vbr, VBR) and value(s, "B PSR: MODE only, IND CLEAR", psr, 0x80000000)
    row("cpu0 setup", "OK" if ok else "BADREGS", "VBR=%s PSR=%s" % (("0x%08x" % vbr) if vbr is not None else "?", ("0x%08x" % psr) if psr is not None else "?"))
    s.send("breakpoint add 0x%08x" % (VBR + 8))

    row("slot0 shadow", "OK" if ok else "BADREGS")
    s.close()

def session_n2(deliver):
    name = "C" if deliver else "D"
    print("=== SESSION %s (-n 2, FREE-RUNNING): %s" % (name, "cpu 1 delivery via MASK1 + SOFT_INT1" if deliver else "CONTROL: MASK1 armed, no SOFT store"))
    s = boot(2)
    if s is None:
        row("two cpus" if deliver else "nodeliver ctl", "NOSTART"); return
    mark = len(s.buf); s.send("machine")
    two = ("cpu1" in s.buf[mark:]) or ("cpu 1" in s.buf[mark:])
    if deliver:
        row("two cpus", "OK" if two else "BADREGS", "the `machine' listing names cpu1" if two else "no cpu1 in the machine listing")
    # cpu 1: vector table, IND clear, parked
    s.send("focus 1"); s.send("VBR=0x%08x" % VBR); s.send("PSR=0x80000000"); s.send("pc=0x%08x" % PARK)
    vbr = s.reg("VBR", cpu=1, cr=True); psr = s.reg("PSR", cpu=1, cr=True); pc1 = s.reg("pc", cpu=1)
    s.send("focus 0")
    if deliver:
        ok = value(s, "C cpu1 VBR", vbr, VBR) and value(s, "C cpu1 PSR: IND clear", psr, 0x80000000) and value(s, "C cpu1 parked", pc1, PARK)
        row("cpu1 setup", "OK" if ok else "BADREGS")
    s.send("r6=0x%08x" % SCRATCH)
    if not store(s, MASK0 + 4, 0x04000000):
        died(s, "mask1 land" if deliver else "nodeliver ctl", "died on the MASK1 store on 2 cpus"); s.close(); return
    g = load(s, MASK0 + 4)
    if deliver:
        row("mask1 land", "OK" if value(s, "C MASK1 readback on 2 cpus (0x04000000 >> 8)", g, 0x00040000, 0x00ffffff) else "BADREGS")
        if not store(s, SOFT0 + 4, 1):
            died(s, "survive", "died on the SOFT_INT1 store on 2 cpus (slot 1 IS connected here)"); s.close(); return
        row("survive", "OK", "the two stores do not kill the host on 2 cpus")
    s.send("pc=0x%08x" % PARK)                     # park cpu 0 too
    s.send("breakpoint add 0x%08x" % (VBR + 8))
    hit = s.cont_until_breakpoint(15.0 if deliver else 8.0)
    if s.dead:
        died(s, "cpu1 deliver" if deliver else "nodeliver ctl", "host died while free-running on 2 cpus"); s.close(); return
    pc1 = s.reg("pc", cpu=1); pc0 = s.reg("pc", cpu=0)
    if deliver:
        if not hit:
            row("cpu1 deliver", "STOP", "no vector entry within 15 s (cpu1 pc=%s)" % (("0x%08x" % pc1) if pc1 is not None else "?"))
            s.close(); return
        row("cpu1 deliver", "OK" if value(s, "C cpu1 pc == VBR+8 (INTERRUPT vector on cpu 1)", pc1, VBR + 8) else "BADREGS")
        psr = s.reg("PSR", cpu=1, cr=True); epsr = s.reg("EPSR", cpu=1, cr=True)
        ok = value(s, "C cpu1 PSR after entry", psr, 0x8000000b) and value(s, "C cpu1 EPSR: the pre-exception PSR", epsr, 0x80000000)
        row("cpu1 shadow", "OK" if ok else "BADREGS")
        row("cpu0 parked", "OK" if value(s, "C cpu0 still parked (it was cpu 1 that took the vector)", pc0, PARK) else "BADREGS")
    else:
        row("nodeliver ctl", "OK" if not hit else "BADREGS", "with nothing pending cpu 1 does NOT enter its vector in 8 s" if not hit else "cpu 1 entered its vector with NOTHING pending")
    s.close()


def main():
    print("BINARY=%s" % BIN)
    session_A()
    prefix = any(r == "HALT" for _, r in rows)
    session_A2()
    session_B()
    session_n2(True)
    session_n2(False)
    ok = sum(1 for _, r in rows if r == "OK")
    print("LUNAABS_RESULT=%d/%d" % (ok, len(rows)))
    halt_rows = [n for n, r in rows if r == "HALT"]
    if halt_rows:
        print("LUNA_ABSENTCPU_WITNESS=PRE_FIX_SYMPTOM (%s)" % ",".join(halt_rows))
    elif fails:
        print("LUNA_ABSENTCPU_WITNESS=FAIL %s" % ",".join(fails))
    else:
        print("LUNA_ABSENTCPU_WITNESS=PASS")
    print("PROBE_WALL=%.1fs" % (time.time() - t0))
    print("LUNAABS_DONE", flush=True)
    sys.exit(0 if not fails else 1)

 *  Definitions related to the Interrupt subsystem.
 */

#include "misc.h"

struct interrupt {
	/*  Functions used to assert and deassert the interrupt.  */
	void		(*interrupt_assert)(struct interrupt *);
	void		(*interrupt_deassert)(struct interrupt *);

	/*
	 *  The interrupt "line" number, or "pin" number, is an internal number
	 *  used by interrupt_assert() and interrupt_deassert(). It may
	 *  correspond to a physical interrupt number, or it may be ignored
	 *  completely.
	 *
	 *  This can either be a small integer corresponding to the interrupt
	 *  pin number, or an entire interrupt mask (for controllers with at
	 *  most 32 interrupts).
	 */
	uint32_t	line;

	/*
	 *  The extra pointer is a pointer to the (conceptual) interrupt
	 *  handler. For a CPU interrupt, this is a pointer to the CPU. For
	 *  e.g. a 8259 interrupt, it is a pointer to the dev_8259 which will
	 *  handle it.
	 */
	void		*extra;

	/*
	 *  Actual name of the interrupt. This is a complete "path", e.g.
	 *  "machine[0].cpu[1].irq[3].isa[14]". It is used for
	 *  connecting/disconnecting, and debug output.
	 */
	char		*name;
};


/*
 *  Macros to make code using the interrupt struct a bit more consise:
 */

#define	INTERRUPT_ASSERT(istruct)	(istruct).interrupt_assert(&(istruct))
#define	INTERRUPT_DEASSERT(istruct)	(istruct).interrupt_deassert(&(istruct))

#define	INTERRUPT_CONNECT(name,istruct)		{		\
	interrupt_handler_lookup(name, &(istruct));		\
	interrupt_connect(&(istruct), 0);			\
	}

#define	INTERRUPT_CONNECT_EXCLUSIVE(name,istruct)	{	\
	interrupt_handler_lookup(name, &(istruct));		\
	interrupt_connect(&(istruct), 1);			\
	}


/*
 *  Registration of interrupt handlers:
 *
 *  Each interrupt handler (i.e. CPUs, interrupt controllers, various bus
 *  controllers) should call interrupt_handler_register() to register itself.
 */

void interrupt_handler_register(struct interrupt *templ);
void interrupt_handler_remove(const char *name);
int interrupt_handler_lookup(const char *name, struct interrupt *templ);


/*
 *  Functions used to keep track of exclusive and non-exclusive users,
 *  respectively, of an interrupt.
 *
 *  If interrupt_connect() returns, it means it succeeded. On failure, the
 *  emulator exits. There may be up to 1 exclusive user and no non-exclusive
 *  users, or 0 exclusive users and any number of non-exclusive users.
 */

void interrupt_connect(struct interrupt *i, int exclusive);
void interrupt_disconnect(struct interrupt *i, int exclusive);


#endif	/*  INTERRUPT_H  */

	case OBIO_CLOCK3:	/*  0x6300000c: Clock ack?  */
		cpunr = (addr - OBIO_CLOCK0) / 4;
		// TODO: Pending counter per cpu?
		if (cpunr == 0 && d->pending_timer_interrupts > 0)
			d->pending_timer_interrupts --;

		reassert_timer_interrupt(d);
		break;

	case INT_ST_MASK0:	/*  0x65000000: Interrupt status CPU 0.  */
	case INT_ST_MASK1:	/*  0x65000004: Interrupt status CPU 1.  */
	case INT_ST_MASK2:	/*  0x65000008: Interrupt status CPU 2.  */
	case INT_ST_MASK3:	/*  0x6500000c: Interrupt status CPU 3.  */
		/*
		 *  From OpenBSD/luna88k machdep.c source code:
		 *
		 *  On write: Bits 31..26 are used to enable/disable levels 6..1.
		 *  On read: Bits 31..29 show value 0-7 of current interrupt.
		 *           Bits 23..18 show current mask.
		 */
		cpunr = (addr - INT_ST_MASK0) / 4;
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			if ((idata & 0x03ffffff) != 0x00000000) {
				/*
				 *  #438: was fatal() + exit(1) -- an ordinary guest
				 *  store to INT_ST_MASK0-3 with any of bits 25..0
				 *  set ended the emulator process.
				 *
				 *  STORE AS-IS, and the reason is not that the low
				 *  bits are dead.  They are not: interrupt_enable[]
				 *  has two readers, and while the interrupt path can
				 *  only ever see bits 26..31, the read path below
				 *  echoes bits 25..8 back at odata[17..0].  Store-as-is
				 *  drops nothing BECAUSE IT STORES EVERYTHING, and the
				 *  echo lands outside both fields this file's own
				 *  comment says a driver reads (31..29, 23..18).
				 *
				 *  Masking would invent a hardware behaviour the tree
				 *  has no source for; INT_SET_LV0..LV7 are defined and
				 *  never read anywhere, the TIMER_EXTERNAL trap again.
				 *  And returning without storing would drop the LEGAL
				 *  prefix in 31..26 too -- a real SPL change -- which
				 *  the guest can read back stale at odata[23..18].
				 *
				 *  The latch is MANDATORY as a capability bound, not
				 *  because a boot pays it: a real boot-to-login makes
				 *  ZERO guarded writes, but the register takes 29,960
				 *  writes/s, so an unlatched complaint is guest-drivable
				 *  at ~2.3 MB/s.
				 */
				if (!d->intmask_lowbits_reported[cpunr]) {
					d->intmask_lowbits_reported[cpunr] = 1;
					fatal("[ luna88k: INT_ST_MASK%i: unimplemented "
					    "low bits in write 0x%08x; stored as-is "
					    "(reported once per cpu) ]\n",
					    cpunr, (uint32_t)idata);
				} else {
					debug("[ luna88k: INT_ST_MASK%i: unimplemented "
					    "low bits in write 0x%08x ]\n",
					    cpunr, (uint32_t)idata);
				}
			}

			d->interrupt_enable[cpunr] = idata;
			reassert_interrupts(d);
		} else {
			uint32_t currentMask = d->interrupt_enable[cpunr];
			uint32_t status = d->interrupt_status & currentMask;
			int highestCurrentStatus = 0;
			odata = currentMask >> 8;
			
			for (int i = 1; i <= 6; ++i) {
				int m = 1 << (25 + i);
				if (status & m)
					highestCurrentStatus = i;
			}

			odata |= (highestCurrentStatus << 29);
			// printf("highest = %i 0x%08x\n", highestCurrentStatus, (int)odata);
		}

		break;

	case SOFT_INT0:		/*  0x69000000: Software Interrupt status CPU 0.  */
	case SOFT_INT1:		/*  0x69000004: Software Interrupt status CPU 1.  */
	case SOFT_INT2:		/*  0x69000008: Software Interrupt status CPU 2.  */
	case SOFT_INT3:		/*  0x6900000c: Software Interrupt status CPU 3.  */
		cpunr = (addr - SOFT_INT0) / 4;

		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			d->software_interrupt_status[cpunr] = idata;
		} else {
			// Reading status clears it.
			odata = d->software_interrupt_status[cpunr];
			d->software_interrupt_status[cpunr] = 0;
		}

		reassert_interrupts(d);
		break;

	case RESET_CPU_ALL:	/*  0x6d000010: Reset all CPUs  */

			cpu->machine->cpus[i]->running = 0;
		break;

# Round #502 m88kexcdeliver -- the MC88100 model stopped EVERY guest CPU for seven trap-like exception classes; now it delivers them (VBR + 8*vector, shadows and PSR per the manual), and a 22-row detector pins the delivery

**2026-09-05.  HIGH.  `src/cpus/cpu_m88k.c` (`m88k_exception()`, 63+/12-), new `regress/m88k_exc_probe.py`, `regress/gate_m88k_rounding.sh` (gate 11).**
Artifacts: `regress/artifacts/m88kexcdeliver/` (both pass briefs, the pass-1 and pass-2 panel answers, the Opus and Fable pass-2 answers,
`PRETABLE_m88kexc.out`, three kill tables, the Opus pass-2 table, every probe output named below).

## The defect

`m88k_exception()` marked only RESET / INTERRUPT / INSTRUCTION_ACCESS / DATA_ACCESS `implemented`; MISALIGNED_ACCESS (4),
UNIMPLEMENTED_OPCODE (5), PRIVILEGE_VIOLATION (6), BOUNDS_CHECK_VIOLATION (7), ILLEGAL_INTEGER_DIVIDE (8), INTEGER_OVERFLOW (9) and
ERROR (10) printed a VERBOSITY_ERROR line and fell to the fallback `vector < M88K_EXCEPTION_USER_TRAPS_START && !implemented`, which
sets `running = false` on EVERY guest CPU: one `divu` by zero, in either mode, ended the emulation.  Real hardware delivers
VBR + 8*vector and the guest OS turns it into a signal.  Pre-existing upstream (the fallback is the 0.7.0 import; `git log -L`
returns only that commit); surfaced by the 2026-09-05 retrospective re-check of #380 (batch 2: GPT-6 Astra read-only, Fable 5.1
adjudicating).  Reachability, MEASURED rather than derived: the instruction raise sites (PRIVILEGE 10 literal sites, ILLEGAL_DIVIDE 4,
OVERFLOW 3, MISALIGNED 1, UNIMPLEMENTED 1) AND a supervisor `tb0/tb1/tcnd`, whose 9-bit field names any vector directly
(`m88k_exception(cpu, ic->arg[2], 1)`), so BOUNDS (7) and ERROR (10) -- which the filing called "latent, no raise site" -- ARE
reachable today (pass 1, Opus: supervisor `tb1 0,r3,7` halted HEAD).  The filing's sentence is corrected in place (ledger, OPEN LIST).

## Witness (rung 3) and fix

`regress/m88k_exc_probe.py`: cold-debugger rows on the COMMITTED, UNMODIFIED `testm88k` machine -- a guest program planted with `put w`,
registers seeded, `br .` at every vector entry, `breakpoint add <entry>`, `continue`, then `reg` / `reg 0,0`.  HEAD `83df95c`:
**0/9** (`probe2_head.out`), **0/10** (`probe10_head.out`), **3/22** with the final instrument (`probe_v3_head.out`: the 3 are the two
rows whose CORRECT answer is HALT and the pre-existing user vector 128); every trap-like class printed `unimplemented vector=` and the
machine stopped.  The fix: **9/9 -> 10/10 -> 22/22** (`probe2_fix.out`, `probe_v3_fix.out`, `probe_v3_fix_final.out` on the
rebuilt tree).

The fix is D1 of the six-member set -- DELIVER, at BOTH sites: `implemented = true` (at VERBOSITY_INFO, the level of the four
already-delivered classes) in the first switch, and a bare `case ... break` for the seven in the tail switch.  **The one-site form was
MEASURED (pass 1, Opus) to halt at the tail `default:` ("internal error") with pc/SXIP/PSR already correct -- only the
machine-still-running bit distinguished it from delivery**, which is why every fault row asserts the STOP channel before pc.  SFU1
(114/115) stays at the fallback by design (row `m88kexcsfu1`); vectors 11..113 and 116..127 (117 with the two SFU1 ones) stay at the
fallback and the detector's negative-control rows pin that they STILL halt.  D4 (make the tail default deliver everything) was named
by the pass-1 panel and not adopted -- bigger blast radius -- filed `m88kexctaildefault`.  Structural consequence, stated in the
comment: with `implemented` = {0..10} and tail labels for {0..10}, the tail `default:` is now UNREACHABLE for vectors < 128; it is
kept as the tripwire for an eighth class.

## The detector, and the four times it was found wanting before it shipped

v1 (9 rows) shipped with the fix build at 16:16.  What each pass found, and what changed -- every score names its log:

| found by | escape (score on the probe of the time) | repair |
|---|---|---|
| table 1 (`killtable/`, 13 mutants, 9 rows) | `M_ERRORDROP` **9/9**: switch 1's ERROR arm is reached ONLY by a trap naming 10 (the re-route enters AFTER switch 1) | row `errortb` (tb1 naming 10); table 2 (`killtable2/`): M_ERRORDROP 9/10, M_ERRORTAIL 8/10, M_REVERT1 9/10 |

 succeeded in 291ms:
		if (machine->physical_ram_in_mb > 64)
			debugmsg(SUBSYS_MACHINE, "memory", VERBOSITY_WARNING,
			    "WARNING: %i MB RAM is more than 64 MB (max on a real LUNA 88K)!",
			    machine->physical_ram_in_mb);
		break;

	case MACHINE_LUNA_88K2:
		machine->machine_name = strdup("LUNA 88K2");
		if (machine->physical_ram_in_mb > 112)
			debugmsg(SUBSYS_MACHINE, "memory", VERBOSITY_WARNING,
			    "WARNING: %i MB RAM is more than 112 MB (max on a real LUNA 88K2)!",
			    machine->physical_ram_in_mb);

		/*  According to OpenBSD source code,
		    the string "MNAME=LUNA88K+" in FUSE_ROM_DATA
		    is used to determine that this is a 88K2, and
		    not an 88K.
	                fuse_rom_data[i] =
	                    (char)((((p->h) >> 24) & 0x000000f0) |
	                           (((p->l) >> 28) & 0x0000000f));
		    where h is first 32-bit word, l is second.
		*/

		for (i = 0; i < strlen(luna88k2_fuse_string); ++i) {
			uint32_t h = luna88k2_fuse_string[i] & 0xf0;
			uint32_t l = luna88k2_fuse_string[i] & 0x0f;
			store_32bit_word(cpu, FUSE_ROM_ADDR + i * 8 + 0, h << 24);
			store_32bit_word(cpu, FUSE_ROM_ADDR + i * 8 + 4, l << 28);
		}

		break;

	default:fatal("Unimplemented LUNA88K machine subtype %i\n",
		    machine->machine_subtype);
		exit(1);
	}

	if (machine->ncpus > 4) {
		fatal("More than 4 CPUs is not supported for LUNA 88K.\n");
		exit(1);
	}

	// Unpause all CPUs. (Normally, the emulator only starts with
	// one CPU running, and it is up to the OS to enable the rest,
	// but on real LUNA-88K machines, all CPUs are started to run
	// the OS kernel code simultaneously and it is up to the OS
	// kernel's initial code to sort things out.)
	for (i = 0; i < (size_t)machine->ncpus; ++i)
		machine->cpus[i]->running = true;

	// machine_add_devices_as_symbols(machine, 0);

	if (!machine->prom_emulation)
		return;

	luna88kprom_init(machine);
}


MACHINE_DEFAULT_CPU(luna88k)
{
	machine->cpu_name = strdup("88100");
}


MACHINE_DEFAULT_RAM(luna88k)
{
	// Two OpenBSD dmesgs found on the Internet for a LUNA-88K2 showed
	// 112 MB of real mem. The max for LUNA-88K was probably 64 MB.
	if (machine->machine_subtype == MACHINE_LUNA_88K2)
		machine->physical_ram_in_mb = 112;
	else
		machine->physical_ram_in_mb = 64;
}


MACHINE_REGISTER(luna88k)
{
	MR_DEFAULT(luna88k, "LUNA88K", MACHINE_LUNA88K);

	machine_entry_add_alias(me, "luna88k");

	machine_entry_add_subtype(me, "LUNA-88K", MACHINE_LUNA_88K,
	    "luna-88k", NULL);

	machine_entry_add_subtype(me, "LUNA-88K2", MACHINE_LUNA_88K2,
	    "luna-88k2", NULL);

	me->set_default_ram = machine_default_ram_luna88k;
}

void interrupt_handler_register(struct interrupt *templ)
{
	int i;

#ifdef INTERRUPT_DEBUG
	printf("interrupt_handler_register(\"%s\")\n", templ->name);
#endif

	/*  See if the name is already registered:  */
	for (i=0; i<nr_of_interrupt_handlers; i++) {
		if (strcmp(templ->name,
		    interrupt_handlers[i].templ.name) != 0)
			continue;

		fatal("\ninterrupt_handler_register(): An interrupt handler"
		    " using the name '%s' is already registered.\n",
		    templ->name);
		exit(1);
	}

	nr_of_interrupt_handlers ++;
	CHECK_ALLOCATION(interrupt_handlers = 
	    (struct interrupt_handler *) realloc(interrupt_handlers,
	    nr_of_interrupt_handlers * sizeof(struct interrupt_handler)));

	interrupt_handlers[nr_of_interrupt_handlers-1].templ = *templ;
	CHECK_ALLOCATION(interrupt_handlers[nr_of_interrupt_handlers-1].
	    templ.name = strdup(templ->name));
}


/*
 *  interrupt_handler_remove():
 *
 *  Remove an interrupt handler from the interrupt subsystem. If there are
 *  still connected users of this interrupt, then an error message is printed
 *  and the emulator aborts.
 */
void interrupt_handler_remove(const char *name)
{
	int i;

#ifdef INTERRUPT_DEBUG
	printf("interrupt_handler_remove(\"%s\")\n", name);
#endif

	for (i=0; i<nr_of_interrupt_handlers; i++) {
		if (strcmp(name, interrupt_handlers[i].templ.name) != 0)
			continue;

		/*
		 *  Remove handler i, and return:
		 */
		if (interrupt_handlers[i].nr_of_exclusive_users > 0 ||
		    interrupt_handlers[i].nr_of_nonexclusive_users > 0) {
			fatal("interrupt_handler_remove(): Attempt to "
			    "remove interrupt handler '%s' which has %i "
			    "exclusive and %i non-exclusive users. Aborting.\n",
			    name, interrupt_handlers[i].nr_of_exclusive_users,
			    interrupt_handlers[i].nr_of_nonexclusive_users);
			exit(1);
		}

		if (i != nr_of_interrupt_handlers-1)
			memcpy(&interrupt_handlers[i],
			    &interrupt_handlers[i + 1],
			    nr_of_interrupt_handlers - i - 1);

		nr_of_interrupt_handlers --;

		return;
	}

	fatal("interrupt_handler_remove(): '%s' not found? Aborting.\n", name);
	exit(1);
}


/*
 *  interrupt_handler_lookup():
 *
 *  Scans the list of registered interrupt handlers for a given name. If the
 *  name is found, the template is filled with valid data, and 1 is returned.
 *  If the name is not found, 0 is returned.
 */
int interrupt_handler_lookup(const char *name, struct interrupt *templ)
{
	int i;

#ifdef INTERRUPT_DEBUG
	printf("interrupt_handler_lookup(\"%s\")\n", name);
#endif

	if (name[0] == '\0') {
		/*  No interrupt:  */
		memset(templ, 0, sizeof(struct interrupt));
		templ->interrupt_assert = no_interrupt_assert;
		templ->interrupt_deassert = no_interrupt_deassert;
	}

	for (i=0; i<nr_of_interrupt_handlers; i++) {
		if (strcmp(name, interrupt_handlers[i].templ.name) != 0)
			continue;

		*templ = interrupt_handlers[i].templ;
		return 1;
	}

	/*  The 'if' is an ugly hack to prevent a Compaq CC warning.  */
	if (i >= nr_of_interrupt_handlers) {
		printf("interrupt_handler_lookup(\"%s\") failed.\n", name);

		printf("Available handler paths are:\n");
		for (i=0; i<nr_of_interrupt_handlers; i++)
			printf("    %s\n", interrupt_handlers[i].templ.name);

		printf("Aborting.\n");
		abort();
	}

	return 0;
}


/*
 *  interrupt_connect():
 *
 *  Increases the exclusive or nonexclusive nr or users of an interrupt.
 */
void interrupt_connect(struct interrupt *in, int exclusive)
{
	int i;

#ifdef INTERRUPT_DEBUG
	printf("interrupt_connect(\"%s\")\n", in->name);
#endif

	if (in->name == NULL || in->name[0] == '\0')
		return;

	for (i=0; i<nr_of_interrupt_handlers; i++) {
		if (strcmp(in->name, interrupt_handlers[i].templ.name) != 0)
			continue;

		if (exclusive) {
			interrupt_handlers[i].nr_of_exclusive_users ++;
			if (interrupt_handlers[i].nr_of_exclusive_users > 1) {
				fatal("Fatal error in interrupt_connect(): "
				    "more than 1 exclusive user. Dumping "
				    "core for backtrace.\n");
				abort();
			}
		} else {
			interrupt_handlers[i].nr_of_nonexclusive_users ++;
		}

		return;
	}

	fatal("Internal error in interrupt_connect(): name '%s' not "
	    "found? Dumping core for debugging.\n", in->name);
	abort();
}


/*
 *  interrupt_disconnect():
 *
 *  Decreases the exclusive or nonexclusive nr or users of an interrupt.
 */
void interrupt_disconnect(struct interrupt *in, int exclusive)
{
	int i;

	if (in->name == NULL || in->name[0] == '\0')
		return;

	for (i=0; i<nr_of_interrupt_handlers; i++) {
		if (strcmp(in->name, interrupt_handlers[i].templ.name) != 0)
			continue;

		if (exclusive) {
			interrupt_handlers[i].nr_of_exclusive_users --;
			if (interrupt_handlers[i].nr_of_exclusive_users < 0) {
				fatal("Fatal error in interrupt_disconnect():"
				    "nr of exclusive users < 0?\n");
				exit(1);
			}
		} else {
			interrupt_handlers[i].nr_of_nonexclusive_users --;
			if (interrupt_handlers[i].nr_of_nonexclusive_users<0) {
				fatal("Fatal error in interrupt_disconnect():"
				    "nr of non-exclusive users < 0?\n");
				exit(1);
			}
		}

		return;
	}

	fatal("Internal error in interrupt_disconnect(): name '%s' not "
	    "found?\n", in->name);
	exit(1);
}




/*
 *  timer_add():
 *
 *  Adds a virtual timer to the list of timers.
 *
 *  Return value is a pointer to a timer struct.
 */
struct timer *timer_add(double freq, void (*timer_tick)(struct timer *timer,
	void *extra), void *extra)
{
	struct timer *newtimer;
	int clamped;

	CHECK_ALLOCATION(newtimer = (struct timer *) malloc(sizeof(struct timer)));

	/*  #427: clamp before anything is stored, so freq and interval agree.  */
	freq = timer_clamp_freq(freq, &clamped);
	if (clamped)
		fatal("[ timer: requested rate is above %f Hz; clamped ]\n",
		    (double) TIMER_MAX_FREQUENCY);

	newtimer->freq = freq;
	newtimer->timer_tick = timer_tick;
	newtimer->extra = extra;

	newtimer->interval = 1.0 / freq;
	newtimer->next_tick_at = timer_current_time + newtimer->interval;

	newtimer->next = first_timer;
	first_timer = newtimer;

	return newtimer;
}


/*
 *  timer_remove():
 *
 *  Removes a virtual timer from the list of timers.
 */
void timer_remove(struct timer *t)
{
	struct timer *prev = NULL, *cur = first_timer;

	while (cur != NULL && cur != t) {
		prev = cur;
		cur = cur->next;
	}

	if (cur == t) {
		if (prev == NULL)
			first_timer = cur->next;
		else
			prev->next = cur->next;
		free(cur);
	} else {
		fprintf(stderr, "attempt to remove timer %p which "
		    "doesn't exist. aborting\n", t);
		exit(1);
	}
}
